Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the automata structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 435. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 938 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_435(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 435. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^938(S, F) is trivial. QED.
